# Agent rust API contract

Source paths are relative to the repository root. Declaration tables identify the interfaces in the source files; runtime behavior is defined by their implementations.

Agent applications use the merged Paxeer BFT network, EVM chain ID `125` (`0x7d`). The kernel participates in block proposal and execution. Block commit establishes finality. Node JSON-RPC uses the root path; hosted methods are described by the [gateway contract](../../../services/platform/hosted/gateway/openrpc.json).

## AI market EIP-1559 preparation

`ai_market::NativeTerms` contains `network_id: u32`, `actor_did: &[u8]`,
`owner_public_key: [u8; 32]`, `account_sequence: u64`, `idempotency_key: [u8; 32]`,
`not_before: u64`, `not_after: u64`, `fee_limit: u128`, `capabilities: &[u8]`,
`access_declaration: &[u8]`, `response_capacity: u32`, and `resources: Resources`,
plus `gas_limit: u64`, `max_fee_per_gas: u128`, and
`max_priority_fee_per_gas: u128`. As in the Python SDK, preparation requires
network 125, zero `not_before`, positive gas, priority fee at most maximum fee,
`not_after` equal to the PAXAI expiry and `idempotency_key` equal to its request ID.
The maximum gas fee must fit the disclosure's `u128` fee bound.

`FinalizedSnapshot::prepare` encodes the request as a `NativeProgramCall` with
ABI 5 and entrypoint `layerx_call`, then ABI-encodes
`call(bytes32,uint16,string,bytes,bytes,bytes,uint32,uint64[7],uint128)` for
`0x000000000000000000000000000000000000101d`. `PreparedOperation` stores the
`Eip1559Call` and its unsigned type-2 RLP (`canonical_bytes()`), with chain ID 125,
nonce from `account_sequence`, the three gas fields, zero value and an empty
access list. It retains the native call, program fee limit, owner key, snapshot
binding, intent and effect for review. `payload_digest()` hashes the encoded
native call with SHA-256.

`review(registry, from)` binds the stored transaction through
`layerx_crypto::payments::bind_call`; the sender is explicit because unsigned RLP
does not encode it. `review_call(registry, call, from)` additionally requires the
supplied call to equal the stored transaction, including nonce and gas terms.
Review checks the decoded native operation, PAXAI domain, intent, effect and
snapshot. `ApprovalTerms::transaction` contains the complete call and sender;
`fee_limit` is the Programs execution budget, while the disclosure fee bound is
`gas_limit * max_fee_per_gas`. Validity and retry identity come from the PAXAI
expiry and request ID. Approval compares every reviewed field, checks the retained
owner key and rebinds the transaction. Signing uses `sign_disclosed_call` and
verifies the recovered EVM sender.

The shared `layerx_crypto::disclosure::call::{encode_program_call,
decode_program_call}` codec uses the Python SDK's fifteen ABI head words and
four ordered dynamic tails. Decoding refuses overflowing integers, invalid native
calls, offsets, padding, truncation and trailing bytes. `ConstructMethod::ProgramCall`
exposes the decoded native call and Programs fee budget; its disclosure uses
Programs ordinal 3 and carries the PAXAI envelope's request ID and expiry.

`OperationRecord::from_signed_transaction(signed_bytes, intent, not_after)` is
the durable record producer. Its identity and idempotency key are the signed
transaction hash, protocol version is 2, network ID is the transaction chain ID,
and `signer_public_key` stores the recovered 20-byte sender left-padded to 32 bytes.
The record retains intent, approved expiry, exact signed bytes, lifecycle/domain
states, attempt count, optional result code, sequence, checkpoint and receipt rung.
Encoding and journal reload preserve these fields and revalidate transaction
identity; submissions retain the exact signed bytes across restart and resend.

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | the agent daemon's node JSON-RPC client over LAYERX_AGENT_NODE_RPC_URL |
| storage | none beyond the agentd store |
| errors | NodeRpcError reports unavailable transport, chain-ID mismatch and wrapped SequencerError values (services/agent/crates/layerx-agentd/src/node_rpc.rs) |
| events | The node RPC client emits no events. |
| gas | none |
| invariants | eth_chainId equals LAYERX_AGENT_NODE_CHAIN_ID before serving |
| pause | none |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.9241</code> | SequencerEndpoint::chain_id returns the eth_chainId quantity as u64 and refuses a non-quantity answer as Malformed (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/sequencer.rs). |
| <code>req.9241</code> | SequencerEndpoint::balance sends eth_getBalance with the address and the pending tag and returns the 32-byte big-endian quantity (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/sequencer.rs). |
| <code>req.9242</code> | SequencerEndpoint::account_proof sends eth_getProof with the address, the slots and the hex height and decodes balance, nonce, code hash, storage hash and every proof node (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/sequencer.rs). |
| <code>req.9242</code> | SequencerEndpoint::history sends px_getHistory with the account, a 32-hex cursor and the limit and refuses the 112-hex cursor form before sending (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/sequencer.rs). |
| <code>req.9243</code> | subscribe sends eth_subscribe with ["logs", filter] and NodeSubscription::next yields each eth_subscription result for the returned id (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/subscription.rs). |
| <code>req.9243</code> | NodeSubscription::close sends eth_unsubscribe with the subscription id for a newHeads subscription (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/subscription.rs). |
| <code>req.9244</code> | PxEndpoint::account and PxEndpoint::balances send px_getAccount and px_getBalances with the account and decode every served field (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/px.rs). |
| <code>req.9244</code> | PxEndpoint::network refuses a px_getNetwork answer that carries an anchor member and list_assets decodes the asset table (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/px.rs). |
| <code>req.9245</code> | SequencerEndpoint::transaction_receipt decodes status, block number, transaction index, gas used and every log of eth_getTransactionReceipt (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/sequencer.rs). |
| <code>req.9245</code> | A null eth_getTransactionReceipt answer is Ok(None) and a status of 0x2 is SequencerError::Malformed. |
| <code>req.9251</code> | account::balance issues eth_call of layerxledger balanceOf (0xb1ffc03e) at the committed height and returns the uint128 amount (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/account.rs). |
| <code>req.9252</code> | module_state::module_state authenticates the derived module slot and its storage witness under a pinned committed header and returns the value bytes (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/module_state.rs). |
| <code>req.9252</code> | module_state::module_state_with_history uses the same witness checks; a mismatched witness address or slot returns ModuleReadError::CommittedChanged (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/module_state.rs). |
| <code>req.9253</code> | history::history returns the px_getHistory page with its next 32-hex cursor (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/history.rs). |
| <code>req.9253</code> | history::did_accounts sends px_getAccount with the DID and returns one account row only when the account is bound to that DID and has an EVM address; otherwise it returns an empty list (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/history.rs). |
| <code>req.9254</code> | payments::list_assets and get_asset decode the px_listAssets table and get_asset refuses an asset id absent from it. |
| <code>req.9254</code> | payments::estimate_fee returns the eth_estimateGas quantity for the call. |
| <code>req.9255</code> | withdrawal::registry builds the module registry from the JSON-RPC fee estimate and asset rows and refuses an absent custody asset with AssetUnavailable. |
| <code>req.9261</code> | submit::submit_signed sends eth_sendRawTransaction with the raw bytes and returns the served hash at rung submitted. |
| <code>req.9261</code> | submit::submit_signed maps a refusal whose data is an LXP_ revert reason to SubmitError with that registry code. |
| <code>req.9262</code> | receipt::lookup_authenticated returns Rung::Receipted for a pax_sequencerReceipt signed by a pinned receipt key. |
| <code>req.9262</code> | receipt::lookup_authenticated reports Rung::Receipted after signature and execution-field checks. receipt::lookup_authenticated_with_commit reports Rung::Committed only after verifying the committed header and receipt coverage against supplied trust and proof evidence (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/receipt.rs). |
| <code>req.9263</code> | stream::subscribe yields one StreamItem::Event per eth_subscription log in order and reports an out-of-order log as a Gap. |
| <code>req.9264</code> | batch::lookup returns the eth_getBlockByNumber block for the height and refuses a null answer. |
| <code>req.9265</code> | evidence::proof_bundle requires a verified committed header to return the landing block, header and verified storage witnesses; a height-only target returns EvidenceError::MissingCommittedHeader (services/agent/crates/layerx-client/src/rpc/evidence.rs). |
| <code>req.9265</code> | evidence::verification_label returns submitted, receipted and committed for the three rungs and no retired level name. |
| <code>req.9266</code> | OperationJournal::submit sends the journalled raw bytes through eth_sendRawTransaction exactly once and resend_exact sends the identical bytes. |
| <code>req.9266</code> | OperationJournal::resolve records Rung::Receipted from the pax_sequencerReceipt answer. |
| <code>req.9267</code> | ObservedSnapshot::finalize with a CommitFinality yields a FinalizedSnapshot exposing the block height, block hash, app hash and store proof digest. |
| <code>req.9267</code> | CommitFinality::new refuses a zero app hash and finalize refuses a commit below the observed height. |
| <code>req.9267</code> | OperationJournal::finalize records the block hash of the commit for an executed record. |
| <code>req.9271</code> | The committed budget decoder decodes every budget version. |
| <code>req.9272</code> | The supply receipt round trip authenticates and refuses tampering. |
| <code>req.9273</code> | SequencerEndpoint::call_at sends the explicit height. |
| <code>req.9274</code> | The committed budget decoder refuses malformed records. |
| <code>req.9276</code> | The committed grant decoder verifies every grant field. |
| <code>req.9277</code> | A JSON-RPC refusal keeps code, message and data. |
| <code>req.9281</code> | SequencerEndpoint::block_by_number decodes full blocks. |
| <code>req.9282</code> | SequencerEndpoint::new refuses plain remote URLs and unpinned https. |

| <code>interface.9.rs-crypto-verifyerror-result-code-j70</code> | <code>rust_pub_item</code> | <code>services/agent/crates/layerx-crypto/src/lib.rs</code> | <code>pub const fn result_code(self) -&gt; ResultCode</code> |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.9321</code> | bind_call on a layerxledger send(bytes) call returns a Disclosure whose counterparties, amounts, asset and idempotency key equal the decoded send payload and whose signing digest is the EIP-1559 signing hash. |
| <code>req.9321</code> | bind_call refuses a call to an address or with a selector outside the construct table. |
| <code>req.9321</code> | bind_call refuses calldata with bytes after the ABI-encoded arguments. |
| <code>req.9322</code> | ledger_payment_call returns an Eip1559Call to 0x101A whose data is send(bytes) over the exact payload and whose disclosure equals bind_call of that call. |
| <code>req.9322</code> | ledger_payment_call refuses a payload the send decoder refuses. |
| <code>req.9323</code> | sign_disclosed_call returns a 65-byte signature from which recover_sender yields the LocalSigner EVM address. |
| <code>req.9323</code> | sign_disclosed_call refuses a disclosure that does not match the call. |
| <code>req.9341</code> | KeystoreSigner refuses a signing request over an activity preimage and signs the bind message. |
| <code>req.9343</code> | The EIP-1559 codec round-trips byte for byte. |

| <code>interface.9.rs-proof-verifiedreceipt-receipt-j70</code> | <code>rust_pub_item</code> | <code>services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/receipt.rs</code> | <code>pub const fn receipt(&amp;self) -&gt; &amp;Receipt</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | layerx-proof receipt, inclusion and complete offline export verification reporting Rung |
| storage | none; pure functions over caller-supplied bytes |
| errors | VerificationFailure with ReceiptCheck, InclusionError, CompleteExportError with RungNotAchieved, ExportCodecError |
| events | none |
| gas | none |
| pause | none |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.9303</code> | AuthorizedBatch::from_receipt_keys selects the sequencer key from the pinned ReceiptKeys list and verify_outcome on that batch reports Rung::Receipted (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/receipt.rs). |
| <code>req.9303</code> | A receipt verified against an authority-supplied key from AuthorizedBatch::new reports Rung::Submitted while level() stays SEQUENCER_SIGNED (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/receipt.rs). |
| <code>req.9303</code> | InclusionEvidence::rung reports Rung::Receipted for activity, receipt and state inclusion (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/inclusion.rs). |
| <code>req.9304</code> | verify_complete with requested Rung::Receipted on a receipt-only artifact returns a report whose achieved value is Rung::Receipted (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/export.rs). |
| <code>req.9304</code> | verify_complete with requested Rung::Committed on an artifact without a certified header is refused with RungNotAchieved naming fact 0, achieved Receipted and requested Committed (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/export.rs). |
| <code>req.9305</code> | The guest response code test authenticates the signed terminal root from pinned activity identity bytes without layerx_wire::activity (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/tests/response_code.rs). |
| <code>req.9305</code> | The terminal v4 test verifies the executed receipt and refuses the mutated leg with fixture-pinned payload hashes (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/tests/terminal_v4.rs). |
| <code>req.9305</code> | The pre-runtime refusal test authenticates the refusal and rejects a flipped activity id with pinned identities (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/tests/pre_runtime_refusal.rs). |
| <code>req.9305</code> | The signed authority history test advances and refuses changed or missing parents from the pinned handover payload (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/tests/signed_authority.rs). |
| <code>req.9308</code> | The paid withdrawal receipt fixture verifies through receipt::verify_outcome and keeps its result code, (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/receipt/withdrawal.rs). |
| <code>req.9309</code> | The complete export decoder refuses an `activity:` fact, a `checkpoint:` fact and a non-empty certificate bucket (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/export_codec.rs). |
| <code>req.9309</code> | export::verify accepts an OfflineExport of receipt and inclusion facts with no settlement domain argument and reports their digests (services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/export.rs). |
| <code>req.9310</code> | The private Evidence token in services/agent/crates/layerx-proof/src/level.rs maps unpinned sequencer signatures to Submitted, and pinned signatures, inclusion and state proofs to Receipted. That token alone cannot authenticate a CometBFT commit. |

| <code>req.9331</code> | RpcClient uses eth_ and hosted px_ methods for state reads and pax_sequencerReceipt for signed execution receipts (services/agent/crates/layerx-sdk/src/rpc.rs). |
| <code>req.9331</code> | send_raw_transaction sends eth_sendRawTransaction with the 0x-prefixed raw bytes and returns the 32-byte hash. |
| <code>req.9331</code> | get_identity_sequence returns the pending transaction count of the address. |
| <code>req.9332</code> | Wallet::send submits one raw EIP-1559 transaction to 0x101A carrying send(bytes) and a 65-byte signature recovering to the wallet address. |
| <code>req.9332</code> | Wallet::open_account encodes openAccount(bytes32) for the asset. |
| <code>req.9332</code> | A revert of a submitted wallet transaction surfaces the registry code and its retriable class. |
| <code>req.9334</code> | verify_receipt accepts the fixture receipt when its signing key is pinned for the receipt height. |
| <code>req.9334</code> | verify_receipt refuses the receipt when the pinned key range excludes its height. |
| <code>req.9342</code> | layerx-sdk exposes no settlement domain or activity codec and its receipt path verifies. |
| <code>req.9344</code> | An agent operation envelope carrying a signed EIP-1559 transaction decodes and binds the recovered sender. |
| <code>req.9345</code> | A program call submitted through the HTTP client is decoded from a raw EIP-1559 transaction and its hash is the transaction hash. |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.9301</code> | VerificationLevel::rung maps UNVERIFIED and SEQUENCER_SIGNED to Rung::Submitted, BATCH_INCLUDED and STATE_PROVEN to Rung::Receipted and CHECKPOINT_FINALISED to Rung::Committed (services/agent/crates/layerx-types/src/verify.rs). |
| <code>req.9301</code> | VerificationLevel::SETTLEMENT_ANCHORED.rung() is None while its wire_rank stays 5 (services/agent/crates/layerx-types/src/verify.rs). |
| <code>req.9302</code> | Every registry section (b) row whose LXP_ code has no header variant classifies by its Retryable column through Retriability::of_revert_reason (services/agent/crates/layerx-types/src/result.rs). |
| <code>req.9302</code> | The revert reason `LXP_BANK_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS: insufficient funds` classifies as Retriable (spec/layerx-merge/contracts/address-table.md:377). |
| <code>req.9311</code> | The checkpoint vectors load and keep every case (services/agent/crates/layerx-types/src/vectors/checkpoint.rs). |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.9312</code> | Every published receipt vector decodes and re-encodes byte for byte (services/agent/crates/layerx-wire/tests/structures.rs). |
| <code>req.9312</code> | The checkpoint vectors keep their pinned header length, header prefix and identities without the settlement document reader (services/agent/crates/layerx-wire/tests/hashing.rs). |
| <code>req.9312</code> | The checkpoint certificate and guarantor attestation domain tags equal the literal version-two tags without the settlement document (services/agent/crates/layerx-wire/tests/hashing.rs). |
